# Changes

This file records completed fixes and software changes so future agents can see what has already been done and avoid repeating work.

For each completed change, add a dated entry with:

- The task ID when one applies, such as `T302`
- A short summary of what changed
- The files or area affected
- The verification performed, such as tests, MCP comparisons, or manual checks

## 2026-06-04

- Character Builder selected-template launch path: the headless external-mesh launch workflow can now accept an explicit native KOTOR template model and label, using that same selected template for both external mesh auto-fit and final `apply_template_rig` binding. This lets Bendak-style automation treat `Bendak.fbx` as the custom payload while `n_mandalorian` remains the KOTOR skeleton/DAG authority instead of falling back to a generic body template. Roadmap task: `T1205` / `T2203`.
  Affected areas: `src/core/characters/headless_body_workflow.py`, `tests/test_headless_body_workflow.py`.
  Ground truth used: MCP `ghostrigger_get_retarget_skeleton_info` refreshed K1 `n_mandalorian` as `N_Mandalorian`, a 70-node native KOTOR model with 18 bones, local `pause1`/`pause2`, and supermodel `S_Female02`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_headless_body_workflow.py::test_t1205_launch_workflow_uses_native_template_without_acurig tests/test_headless_body_workflow.py::test_t1205_launch_workflow_uses_selected_native_template_for_fit_and_bind -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_bendak_template_launch`; `python -m pytest tests/test_headless_body_workflow.py::test_external_model_normalization_uses_bone_landmarks_for_front_axis tests/test_headless_body_workflow.py::test_external_model_normalization_snaps_to_selected_reference_frame tests/test_headless_body_workflow.py::test_normalization_persists_fit_report_in_model_metadata tests/test_character_builder_template_rig.py::test_apply_template_rig_strips_imported_armature_and_clears_old_skin -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_bendak_template_fit_smoke`; `python -m py_compile src/core/characters/headless_body_workflow.py tests/test_headless_body_workflow.py`.

- Character Builder bind-provenance export gate: export preflight now blocks `native_template_final` rigs that do not carry explicit, matching native-base and imported-payload bind provenance. Staged validation reports now include the full `character_builder_bind` block alongside rig-state evidence, so Bendak-style exports can show that `Bendak.fbx` is the payload while `n_mandalorian` is the native KOTOR skeleton authority. - Character Builder native DAG fingerprint evidence: NativeSkeletonSnapshot now exposes a deterministic SHA-256 fingerprint payload for the selected KOTOR node DAG, excluding volatile filesystem paths while retaining exact node paths, roles, hook categories, game/supermodel facts, and selected variant metadata. - LordVader fork sync and Qt branch release-readiness pass: Merged the latest `partner/qt-ghostrigger` changes, preserved the new split renderer/viewport facades, kept local Character Builder/BAS/retarget/lightsaber work in the recovered tree, made FBX backend selection explicit so Autodesk SDK requests no longer silently fall back to Blender unless a caller opts in, refreshed the README for the Qt branch/modder workflows, and simplified `build.bat` around `requirements.txt` plus the PyInstaller spec. - Camera authoring translate stability fix: Live camera gizmo translate now preserves the existing camera rotation instead of copying transient helper-node rotation back into the camera DTO each mouse move, and active scene cameras no longer force the perspective viewport through the camera unless camera view is actually active. - Sequence Editor lane keying and camera-cut preview fix: Added individual transform-property lanes for position/rotation/scale components, exposed transform/camera/light lane submenus in the Sequence track-tree context menu, routed Key and Auto Key through the selected lane when one is active, and connected camera/light panel edits to auto-key existing transform/property lanes. The timeline now includes matching master/binding/track rows so keys sit on the same lane as the left-side Sequence/Type tree, preserves expansion state across refreshes, and uses layout-driven equal row/header heights. Sequence playback now marks the preview viewport as animation playback so camera helper/wire overlays redraw on every evaluated frame, sequence evaluation forces camera/overlay dirty redraws, and perspective view no longer switches to the first bound camera unless an explicit Camera Cut track is active. ## 2026-05-30

- Character Builder bone-landmark import fit and bind tagging: External mesh normalization now prefers a humanoid bone-landmark frame when the imported mesh and selected KOTOR base both expose enough skeleton data. The fit derives up/right/front from pelvis/head/left-right limb landmarks, maps that frame onto the native KOTOR base frame, and falls back to the existing bounds-based KOTOR-space normalization for static/non-skeletal imports. Template rig application now tags the generated model and imported mesh payloads as bound to the native KOTOR skeleton after bone-map/qbone/tbone/skin rows are created. No roadmap task ID applies.

  Affected areas: `src/systems/bas/README.md`, `src/systems/bas/models/README.md`, `src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py`, BAS regression coverage.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `python -m pytest -q tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_runtime_contract_is_documented_and_guarded tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_wgpu_bas_attachment_follows_animated_socket_matrix tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_wgpu_bas_head_skin_uses_animated_socket_root_not_head_node_offset tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_moderngl_bas_head_skin_uses_root_local_vbo_and_socket_draw_matrix tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_head_skin_uses_attachment_root_local_bind_space_when_weapon_is_added` passed 5 tests.

- Body Attachment System D3D live socket-follow fix: WGPU/D3D12 BAS attachment matrices now recompute from the current animation pose each draw instead of reusing the render-queue's cached bind-time world matrix. BAS skin meshes use the animated BAS root/socket matrix, while rigid weapon/attachment meshes use their own animated socket-following node matrix, so heads and equipped weapons move with `headhook`, `rhand`, and `lhand` during body playback rather than staying static. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py`, BAS/WGPU regression coverage.
  Verification: KotorMCP ground-truth checks with `compare_model_pipelines`, `inspect_mdl`, and `inspect_mdl_ghostrigger` confirmed K1 `p_carthbb`, `pmha01`, `w_blstrpstl_001`, and `w_vbroshort_001` still match the PyKotor pipeline. `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `python -m pytest -q tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_wgpu_bas_attachment_follows_animated_socket_matrix tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_wgpu_bas_head_skin_uses_animated_socket_root_not_head_node_offset tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_moderngl_bas_head_skin_uses_root_local_vbo_and_socket_draw_matrix tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_head_skin_uses_attachment_root_local_bind_space_when_weapon_is_added` passed 4 tests. Visible WGPU/D3D12 app harness loaded `K1:P_CarthBB`, played looping `walk`, attached `pmha01`, `w_blstrpstl_001`, and `w_vbroshort_001`, and confirmed non-zero matrix deltas between two animation samples for the head and both weapon layers. Report/screenshots: `artifacts/bas_carth_live_socket_follow_20260529/wgpu_d3d12/report.json`, `artifacts/bas_carth_live_socket_follow_20260529/wgpu_d3d12/t1_viewport.png`, and `artifacts/bas_carth_live_socket_follow_20260529/wgpu_d3d12/t2_viewport.png`. A ModernGL visual smoke captured `artifacts/bas_carth_live_socket_follow_20260529/modern_gl/t2_viewport.png`.

- Body Attachment System renderer parity fix: ModernGL/OpenGL now treats BAS head skin meshes the same way as WGPU: the head skin VBO is built in attachment-root local bind space, the BAS root follows the body `headhook` socket at draw time, and BAS skin layers are excluded from the ModernGL GPU skinning shader/palette path. This fixes the Carth body + PMHA01 head + weapon case where OpenGL first stretched the head surface, then hid it behind only rigid mouth/eye parts when attachments were present. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py`, `src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py`, BAS regression coverage.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `python -m pytest -q tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_moderngl_bas_head_skin_uses_root_local_vbo_and_socket_draw_matrix tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_head_skin_uses_attachment_root_local_bind_space_when_weapon_is_added tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_wgpu_bas_attachment_uses_prepared_world_matrix_during_animation` passed 3 tests. Visible app harnesses loaded `K1:P_CarthBB`, played looping `walk`, attached `pmha01`, `w_blstrpstl_001`, and `w_vbroshort_001`, and captured both renderer paths with fallback disabled: ModernGL report/screenshot at `artifacts/bas_carth_walk_backend_fix_20260529/modern_gl/report.json` and `artifacts/bas_carth_walk_backend_fix_20260529/modern_gl/modern_gl_viewport.png`; WGPU/D3D12 report/screenshot at `artifacts/bas_carth_walk_backend_fix_20260529/wgpu_d3d12/report.json` and `artifacts/bas_carth_walk_backend_fix_20260529/wgpu_d3d12/wgpu_d3d12_viewport.png`. Both reports confirmed active body animation on `P_CarthBB` and zero deltas from head/weapon attachment roots to their sockets.

- Body Attachment System model recipe persistence: The BAS toolbox now has a `Save Build` button that prompts for a build/model name and writes a named lightweight JSON build into `src/systems/bas/models`. BAS JSON preserves the body, layer order, attached head/weapon resrefs, socket targets, per-layer local position/rotation/scale transforms for future alignment tweaks, and runtime rules for socket-following attachments without copying mesh, texture, animation, MDL, or MDX data. Opening a BAS build `.json` through the normal model-open flow reimports it as a composed preview model resolved from game resrefs. The BAS models README now documents the build schema and folder purpose. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/systems/bas/model_recipe.py`, `src/systems/bas/models/README.md`, `src/gui/panels/qt_body_attachment_panel.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, BAS regression coverage.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/systems/bas/model_recipe.py src/gui/panels/qt_body_attachment_panel.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `pytest -q tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_exposes_save_build_signal tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_attachment_socket_layer_follows_body_dummy_without_skinning tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_model_recipe_preserves_body_layers_sockets_and_resrefs tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_saves_bas_model_recipe_into_system_models_dir tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_loads_bas_recipe_as_composed_preview_model tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_wgpu_bas_attachment_uses_prepared_world_matrix_during_animation tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_head_skin_uses_attachment_root_local_bind_space_when_weapon_is_added` passed 7 tests. Pytest emitted a Windows temp symlink cleanup warning after the successful run.

- Body Attachment System WGPU head visibility fix: WGPU now honors the precomputed BAS attachment world matrix for socket-following attachment meshes while body animation is active, instead of recomputing the attachment node's animated matrix. This prevents BAS head skin rows from being double-transformed or culled after the head bind-space stabilization, restoring the full head surface instead of leaving only rigid mouth/eye parts visible. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py`, BAS/WGPU regression coverage.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/systems/bas/model_recipe.py src/gui/panels/qt_body_attachment_panel.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `pytest -q tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_exposes_save_build_signal tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_model_recipe_preserves_body_layers_sockets_and_resrefs tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_saves_bas_model_recipe_into_system_models_dir tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_loads_bas_recipe_as_composed_preview_model tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_wgpu_bas_attachment_uses_prepared_world_matrix_during_animation tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_head_skin_uses_attachment_root_local_bind_space_when_weapon_is_added` passed 6 tests. Pytest emitted a Windows temp symlink cleanup warning after the successful run.

- Body Attachment System head bind-space stabilization: BAS head skin layers now keep their head/tongue vertices in attachment-root local bind space while the BAS root follows the body socket. This keeps attached heads isolated from the body animation palette without leaving head skin meshes in large negative skin-local coordinates with compensating full-node matrices, which made head deformation look wrong when additional weapon layers were added. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py`, BAS regression coverage.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py src/core/animation/gpu_skinning.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `python -m pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_kmax_scene_composite_preserves_authored_root_name_for_animation_skinning tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_kmax_scene_composite_keeps_bas_layers_out_of_body_dfs_indices tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_resets_stale_camera_node_wrapper_before_attaching_layers tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_exposes_bas_slots_and_attach_signal tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_tracks_attachment_layers tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_attachment_preview_parents_item_to_body_socket tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_attachment_socket_layer_follows_body_dummy_without_skinning tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_head_skin_uses_attachment_root_local_bind_space_when_weapon_is_added tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_preview_targets_body_scene_object_not_arbitrary_selection tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_preview_applies_as_layer_and_restores_animation_pose tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_animation_source_model_keeps_bas_body_as_animation_owner tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_animation_engine_returns_to_body_without_losing_time tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_layer_change_does_not_reload_body_animation_panel tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_layer_change_refreshes_matching_attachment_animation_panel tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_scene_object_activation_preserves_body_animation_panel tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_ignores_anatomical_hand_placeholders -q` passed 16 tests. Real K1 `P_CarthBB` + `pmha01` probes confirmed head-only and head+`w_lghtsbr_001` now produce identical head/tongue bind-space positions and the body palette remains at 87 nodes with no attachment names for both single-weapon and dual-weapon cases. Probe report: `artifacts/bas_weapon_animation_matrix_20260529/head_bind_space_after_fix.json`.

- Body Attachment System socket-layer attachment fix: Changed BAS preview composition so heads and equipment are isolated attachment layers that follow body dummy sockets (`headhook`, `lhand`, `rhand`) at render time instead of being physically grafted into the animated body skeleton. BAS attachment skin meshes are excluded from the body animation skin palette, preventing duplicate head/body bone names from exploding the body mesh when Body animations play. WGPU render-data extraction now keeps BAS skin attachment vertices local and drives placement through the socket-following model matrix. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py`, `src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py`, `src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py`, `src/core/animation/gpu_skinning.py`, BAS/animation regression coverage.
  Verification: inspected real K1 `P_CarthBB` and `pmha01` metadata from the installed game and confirmed the body exposes `headhook`, `lhand`, `rhand`, `Lhand_g`, and `Rhand_g` sockets while the head keeps its own hierarchy; `python -m py_compile src/core/animation/gpu_skinning.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `python -m pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_resets_stale_camera_node_wrapper_before_attaching_layers tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_exposes_bas_slots_and_attach_signal tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_tracks_attachment_layers tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_attachment_preview_parents_item_to_body_socket tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_attachment_socket_layer_follows_body_dummy_without_skinning tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_preview_targets_body_scene_object_not_arbitrary_selection tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_preview_applies_as_layer_and_restores_animation_pose tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_animation_source_model_keeps_bas_body_as_animation_owner tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_animation_engine_returns_to_body_without_losing_time tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_layer_change_does_not_reload_body_animation_panel tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_layer_change_refreshes_matching_attachment_animation_panel tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_scene_object_activation_preserves_body_animation_panel tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_ignores_anatomical_hand_placeholders -q` passed 13 tests. Visible full-window harness loaded `K1:P_CarthBB`, attached `pmha01`, played `pause2`, confirmed the engine stayed on the body, no BAS attachment mesh entered skinning, and the head rendered on the neck socket without body deformation. - Body Attachment System animation preservation: Kept BAS attachments as layered viewport previews while preserving the loaded body as the Body animation source. Scene refresh and BAS layer changes no longer reload the Body animation panel or move playback onto the temporary composite preview; active body playback keeps its selected animation, time, and playing state while attached weapons remain visible in the preview/scene runtime. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, BAS/animation regression coverage.

  Follow-up: Fixed `P_CarthBB` weapon layers disappearing after BAS attach. The root cause was a copied viewport camera `all_nodes()` wrapper: after `P_CarthBB` had been loaded in the retarget target viewport, BAS deep-copied a body whose node traversal still pointed at the pre-attachment body graph, so the weapon attached to a stale traversal and vanished when the preview reloaded. BAS now resets copied model traversal before composing layers, copied attachment models get the same reset, and `CameraManager` refreshes generated camera/light wrappers from the current model instance instead of preserving stale copied wrappers. Ground-truth checks confirmed `K2:P_CarthBB` has `lhand`/`rhand` hooks and `K2:w_vbroshort_001` has the expected weapon mesh nodes. Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/camera/camera_manager.py src/gui/panels/qt_body_attachment_panel.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `python -m pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_resets_stale_camera_node_wrapper_before_attaching_layers tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_exposes_bas_slots_and_attach_signal tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_body_attachment_panel_tracks_attachment_layers tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_attachment_preview_parents_item_to_body_socket tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_preview_targets_body_scene_object_not_arbitrary_selection tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_preview_applies_as_layer_and_restores_animation_pose tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_animation_source_model_uses_bas_composite_preview_for_body_source tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_animation_engine_moves_to_layered_preview_without_losing_time tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_bas_ignores_anatomical_hand_placeholders -q` passed 9 tests. ## 2026-05-28

- Content Browser subcategory taxonomy: Added game-style subcategory metadata and nested navigation folders for Inventory, Weapons, and Placeables. Inventory rows now sort into Spikes, Traps, Medkits, Masks, Implants, Gauntlets, Armbands, Droid Items, Belts, Stims, and Misc Items; weapon rows sort into Grenades, Lightsabers, Vibroblades, Blasters, Blaster Rifles, Single-Handed Weapons, Two-Handed Weapons, and Misc Weapons; placeables sort into Containers, Computers & Panels, Doors & Transitions, Furniture, Lights & VFX, Traps & Hazards, Environmental Props, and Misc Placeables. The unified Content Browser exposes nested category folders and tag filters for these groups, and the Module Editor asset browser shows/filter category paths. No roadmap task ID applies.

- Sprite Materials toolbox and viewport alpha rendering: Added a dockable `QtSpriteMaterialPanel` for inspecting and editing alpha-card/sprite-like mesh materials used by fur/hair, foliage, window/glass, decal, glow, and lightsaber blade surfaces. The panel detects sprite candidates from runtime mesh/TXI fields and texture/name hints, supports candidate filtering, visibility/isolation, runtime mode edits for opaque/cutout/blended/additive/lighten rendering, cutoff/opacity/decal/key-black-matte/glow controls, and original-value reset. The main window exposes the dock through the Modules menu and command strip, syncs it to the active viewport model, selects meshes in the viewport/Module Meshes panel, invalidates renderer alpha/pass caches after edits, persists per-model sprite overrides in `config/sprite_material_overrides.json`, and registers the new `spriteMaterials` layout id / `sprite_materials` dock key in packaged layouts and theme/layout docs. The WGPU material path now promotes transparency hints to cutout queues, auto-keys saber/blade matte cards, carries sprite alpha/glow/material-quality controls in the mesh uniform, and keeps Realistic/Shaded/Flat shader behavior distinct: Realistic gets glow and full material tone, Shaded preserves alpha/cutouts without forcing matte cards opaque, and Flat avoids the heavier effects while still using the alpha queue. No roadmap task ID applies.

## 2026-05-27

- T2406 Mixamo full-body PMBAM gesture correction: The verified Mixamo -> PMBAM retarget profile now uses the full PMBAM humanoid exact-segment correction policy by default, including spine->chest and clavicle->upperarm pairs. This fixes force/spell/combat clips such as `Standing 2H Magic Attack 01.fbx` where the previous limb-only policy left the target shoulders and upper torso disconnected from the source gesture. Explicit legacy profiles can still request `mixamo_limb_only`.

- T2406 Mixamo source-rotation retarget correction: The verified Mixamo -> PMBAM path now declares Blender-FBX identity source quaternion conversion and source-rest reference policy, and the R3.B Aurora writer honors that conversion instead of applying the UE5 X/Y quaternion mirror to Mixamo clips. - Bastila lower-limb animation fix: Protected the stock `P_BastilaBB` headless-body import path so its `torso` skin node normalizes the transposed lower-limb compact palette slots to the matching thigh, shin, foot, and toe bones used by `P_BastilaBB02`, Juhani, and other K1 party bodies. The correction now happens in the loader before weights bind, so OpenGL, D3D, export, and diagnostics all consume the same corrected `bone_map`; the GPU renderer stays on the normal DFS-indexed G5 qBone/tBone path and no renderer-level `slot_bone_remap` override is used. Animation entries now label their effective source as `local`, `inherited`, or `override` so Bastila head/body clips can be diagnosed clearly. No roadmap task ID applies.

## 2026-05-25

- Stage 9A WGPU backend restart guard: Added a Settings save check for explicit WGPU backend-type changes because `WGPU_BACKEND_TYPE` cannot be changed after a WGPU device exists. Switching ModernGL/OpenGL and WGPU Direct3D 12 remains a normal hot swap, but switching an active WGPU Direct3D 12 session to WGPU Vulkan or WGPU OpenGL now shows a restart-required message, saves the selected renderer, and automatically relaunches GhostRigger from the current command line after the existing dirty-scene prompt allows shutdown. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/dialogs/qt_settings_dialog.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, renderer backend settings regression coverage.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/dialogs/qt_settings_dialog.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_settings_dialog_persists_explicit_renderer_backend_choice tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_settings_dialog_uses_prestart_hardware_snapshot tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_settings_dialog_restart_only_for_explicit_wgpu_backend_type_changes tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_settings_dialog_flags_auto_restart_for_explicit_wgpu_backend_type_switch tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_renderer_capability_snapshot_labels_unavailable_options -q`.

- Stage 9A Settings launch hang fix: Moved Hardware tab CPU/GPU diagnostics and renderer-backend availability probing out of the Settings dialog construction path and into the pre-start splash path. Startup now captures cached hardware and renderer capability snapshots while prelaunch checks are already running, passes them into the main window, and the Settings Hardware tab / Renderer combo only display those cached snapshots. This avoids blocking Settings while Windows CIM/WMIC, `py-cpuinfo`, ModernGL, or WGPU backend probes run. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/dialogs/qt_settings_dialog.py`, `src/gui/rendering/hardware_info.py`, `src/gui/rendering/renderer_capabilities.py`, settings dialog regression coverage.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/dialogs/qt_settings_dialog.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py src/gui/rendering/hardware_info.py src/gui/rendering/renderer_capabilities.py tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_settings_dialog_persists_explicit_renderer_backend_choice tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_settings_dialog_uses_prestart_hardware_snapshot -q`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_renderer_capability_snapshot_labels_unavailable_options -q`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_stage9_performance.py::test_hardware_diagnostics_detector_never_requires_cpuinfo tests/test_renderer_stage9_performance.py::test_hardware_diagnostics_normalizes_intel_arch_and_gpu_fallback -q`.

- Stage 9A Hardware tab detection fix: Added `py-cpuinfo>=9.0.0` to the Python requirements and hardened the Settings Hardware tab detector so Intel x86-64 CPUs display as `Intel64 (x86-64)` instead of raw Windows `AMD64`, SIMD capability flags populate from `py-cpuinfo` with Windows feature fallbacks, and GPU adapter names fall back to Windows video-controller discovery when renderer diagnostics do not expose the adapter. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `requirements.txt`, `src/gui/rendering/hardware_info.py`, `tests/test_renderer_stage9_performance.py`.
  Verification: `python -m pip install -r requirements.txt`; `python -m pip show py-cpuinfo`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/hardware_info.py src/gui/dialogs/qt_settings_dialog.py src/gui/panels/qt_diagnostics_panel.py tests/test_renderer_stage9_performance.py`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_stage9_performance.py -q`; direct `collect_hardware_diagnostics(renderer_diagnostics={"backend_id": "wgpu_d3d12"}, target_fps=60)` probe reported `Intel(R) Core(TM) i7-6700 CPU @ 3.40GHz`, `Intel64 (x86-64)`, SSE/SSE2/SSE3/SSSE3/SSE4.1/SSE4.2/AES/AVX/AVX2/FMA, and `NVIDIA GeForce GTX 1060 6GB; Intel(R) HD Graphics 530`.

- Stage 9A hover/navigation jitter mitigation: Cleared mesh-hover state when camera navigation starts, when mouse-wheel zoom changes the camera, when the viewport is left, and when transform gizmo dragging begins so stale hover outlines no longer reproject/flicker during orbit, pan, zoom, or gizmo movement. Hover redraw requests now use a `mesh hover changed` reason instead of the unrelated snap-view animation reason. No roadmap task ID applies.

- WGPU GPU skinning path: Added a WGPU skinned mesh shader and storage-buffer bone-palette binding so animated skin vertices are deformed in WGSL instead of through the CPU fallback. WGPU mesh buffers now use a superset vertex layout with position, normal, UV0/UV1, four bone indices, and four weights; animated WGPU render-data requests keep expanded bind-space rows and disable CPU skinning; per-skin `MatrixPaletteUploader` instances are cached by WGPU resources and only upload compact 128-matrix palettes as animation poses change. WGPU capabilities/diagnostics now report GPU skinning support, storage-buffer palettes, zero CPU skinning fallback, uploaded matrix counts, and ready skinned pipelines. ModernGL/OpenGL skinning remains unchanged. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py`, `src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py`, `src/gui/rendering/shaders/wgpu_mesh_skinned.wgsl`, `tests/test_wgpu_stage7_render_data.py`, `tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py`.
  Verification: direct MCP-helper call `compare_model_pipelines('K1', 'n_bith')` matched with 75 PyKotor/GhostRigger nodes and no discrepancies; `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py tests/test_wgpu_stage7_render_data.py tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py`; `pytest tests/test_wgpu_stage7_render_data.py -q`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_wgpu_capabilities_report_display_mode_limits tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py::test_wgpu_edge_indices_are_deduplicated_for_edged_faces -q`; `pytest tests/test_regression.py::test_gpu_skin_bone_id_attribute_contract_is_integer tests/test_regression.py::test_gpu_skin_bone_ids_split_to_int32_buffer -q`; offscreen WGPU pipeline smoke created textured and skinned pipelines with `gpu_skinning=ready`; real `K1:n_bith` walk-pose WGPU resource smoke uploaded four skin palette buffers, reported 16 uploaded bone matrices, and had zero CPU fallback rows.

- WGPU skinned animation deformation follow-up: Changed the WGPU CPU skinning fallback to skin the same expanded triangle-list/VBO rows that ModernGL uploads, preserving per-face UV/material row layout while reusing GhostRigger's existing `MatrixPaletteUploader` palette math for positions and normals. Animated skin nodes now request bind-space VBO rows with `apply_skin_node_transform_for_bind=False`, carry the VBO bone IDs/weights through renderer-neutral `MeshRenderData`, and avoid collapsing animated skin back to the original indexed vertex list. This fixes shredded WGPU animated meshes such as `K1:n_bith` while leaving ModernGL/OpenGL unchanged. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py`, `src/gui/rendering/skeleton_render_data.py`.

- T501/T1204 Character Builder skeleton attachment, symmetry, and supermodel library readiness: Kept Character Builder mesh placement tied to the whole character by promoting selected mesh transform-gizmo drags to the model root only inside the Character Builder viewport, so the generated skeleton does not stay behind when the imported mesh is positioned. Restored symmetry for transform-gizmo bone translation and let Character Builder guide persistence honor the viewport Symmetry toggle. Generated ROM motion assignments now preserve the selected supermodel, allowing Load Library to list real inherited clips such as `S_Male02` even while ROM preview remains selected.

No-UV stock blade variants such as `w_lghtsbr_008` are no longer mistaken for deformation helpers; their sparse segmented blade strips now render through a smooth preview quad derived from the authored local blade bounds, keeping MDL data untouched while producing a vibrant emissive game-style blade.

- Lightsaber blade preview support: Added a shared lightsaber blade material detector, allowed Aurora scale controllers to evaluate to `0.0`, and wired animated scale plus additive fallback rendering into the viewport renderer paths so short-saber `powered`, `off`, `powerup`, and `powerdown` animation blocks can visibly extend/retract the blade. The missing/neutral-mask texture path now generates a colored blade mask with transparent edges, infers the yellow K2 short-saber variant from `w_shortsbr_009`/`LghtSbr09`, and synthesizes UVs for the two K2 blade planes that omit UV arrays so they no longer render as solid white panels. No roadmap task ID applies.

- Test-run policy update: Documented that agents must default to targeted checks only and must not run broad suites (`pytest tests/`, `pytest tests/ -x`, `pytest tests/ -m "not slow"`) or full-scan validations unless the user explicitly approves them for that task. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `AGENTS.md`.
  Verification: Documentation-only change; no tests run.

- WGPU renderer pass 3: Added a real viewport surface host that hot-swaps renderer-owned widgets, embeds WGPU's `rendercanvas.qt.QRenderWidget` as the live viewport surface, keeps ModernGL/Null on the existing QLabel/PIL image path, and forwards input events from the active surface back to the viewport controller. WGPU now has a renderer-owned mesh data adapter/cache, depth buffer, basic untextured mesh pipeline with material colour, and preserves the WGPU grid. ModernGL/OpenGL remains the complete fallback renderer. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/viewports/viewport_host.py`, `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py`, `src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py`, renderer interface/factory/adapters, `src/gui/qt_lib.py`.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/viewports/viewport_host.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/rendering/renderer_interface.py src/gui/rendering/renderer_factory.py src/gui/rendering/moderngl_renderer.py src/gui/rendering/null_renderer.py src/gui/rendering/mesh_render_data.py src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py src/gui/qt_lib.py`; `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py -q`; `pytest tests/test_theme_layout_loading.py -q`; `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py tests/test_regression.py -q`; WGPU Auto viewport smoke confirmed live `QRenderWidget`, surface host input bridge, depth/mesh/grid pipelines ready; synthetic triangle mesh smoke passed on WGPU Direct3D 12, Vulkan, and OpenGL with one uploaded mesh and one rendered triangle; ModernGL smoke rendered through `QLabel` with GL 330; actual `python main.py` Qt startup sanity check produced no fatal/error log entries. Broader `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -q` was attempted but timed out after 124 seconds.

- WGPU renderer pass 2: Replaced the diagnostic-only WGPU adapter with a real `wgpu-py` / `rendercanvas.qt.QRenderWidget` backend that probes Qt surface/device/clear-present availability in isolated subprocesses, creates a lazy WGPU device/context for the selected backend, renders the theme-aware clear pass plus WGPU grid lines, reports adapter/format diagnostics, and guards `WGPU_BACKEND_TYPE` so backend changes require restart after a WGPU device exists. ModernGL/OpenGL remains the full scene renderer and fallback; WGPU mesh and texture scene rendering remain future work. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py`.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/wgpu_renderer.py tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py -q`; WGPU Auto/Direct3D 12/Vulkan/OpenGL smoke renders to 96x64 through `QRenderWidget`; renderer capability snapshot reports all WGPU modes available on the test Windows/NVIDIA machine; `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py -q`; direct `QtGhostRiggerMainWindow` launch smoke showed the real main window and exited cleanly with current ModernGL settings.

- Renderer backend selection layer: Added a renderer abstraction, backend enum/capability/settings records, a factory/fallback proxy, ModernGL adapter, WGPU diagnostic adapter, Direct3D experimental unavailable adapter, and Null Diagnostic renderer. The Qt viewport now creates renderers through the factory while preserving the existing ModernGL/OpenGL 3.3 renderer as the working fallback; settings.json gains safe renderer defaults, the Settings dialog persists an explicit renderer mode choice, and the main, Retarget, and Unreal Animator viewports consume that application-level setting. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/`, `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/dialogs/qt_settings_dialog.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_retarget_window.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_unreal_animator.py`, `src/gui/qt_lib.py`, `requirements.txt`, `tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py`.
  Verification: `python -m compileall -q src/gui/rendering src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py src/gui/dialogs/qt_settings_dialog.py tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py`; `pytest tests/test_renderer_backend_selection.py -q`; `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py -q`; `pytest tests/test_theme_layout_loading.py -q`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py -q -k "settings or renderer or gpu or grid"`.

No roadmap task ID applies.

- T1403 follow-up: Added per-profile dock layout overrides so user changes to the selected visual profile persist across restarts. Opening, closing, moving, or tabbing dock tools now records panel visibility/region/size and visible dock groups under `theme_layout.layout_overrides[selected_layout]`; `LayoutManager` merges those overrides into the packaged/user XML profile when the profile is loaded. This lets a user add a dock such as Nodes to Clean and have Clean retain that dock on next startup without modifying packaged layout XML.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/libtheme/theme_settings.py`, `src/gui/libtheme/layout_manager.py`, `src/gui/libtheme/layout_applier.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `tests/test_theme_layout_loading.py`.

## 2026-05-23

- Fixed Retarget Workbench MDL export for game-library-loaded target models. KOTOR target models loaded from the Game Library now keep transient original MDL/MDX resource bytes, and the retarget preview exporter stages those source bytes inside the `ExportJob` transaction before injecting the assigned animation. This preserves the original game-resource mesh/skin payload while avoiding the previous "no source MDL path" error for in-memory PMBAM targets. Roadmap reference: T2406.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/retarget_preview_export.py`, `src/core/assets/resource_manager.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `tests/test_retarget_preview_export.py`.
  Verification: MCP PMBAM resource lookup refreshed before touching MDL/export code; `python -m pytest tests/test_retarget_preview_export.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_export_source`; `python -m py_compile src/core/retargeting/retarget_preview_export.py src/core/assets/resource_manager.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_retarget_preview_export.py`.

- Fixed the animated skinned-mesh hover/selection outline path so GPU-rendered PMBAM previews and the blue/cyan mesh hover overlay use the same skinning contract. The Qt overlay now computes animated skin vertices through `MatrixPaletteUploader` with the same per-skin local palette used by the visible GPU draw, instead of falling back to the legacy CPU LBS path that could fold/stretch the hover outline while the textured mesh looked correct. Roadmap reference: T2402/T2406.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py`, `tests/test_regression.py`.

- Fixed the Animation Retargeting Workbench FBX source viewport integration. Imported UE/FBX source animation clips now build a lightweight dummy-node preview model for the workbench source viewport, preserving imported node names/casing, parent hierarchy, rest/global positions, prepared render bounds, and bone/joint overlay visibility. The import handler now passes the loaded `SourceSkeletonClip` to the workbench window instead of only updating metadata labels, so animation-only FBX clips no longer leave the source viewport on "Empty Scene." Roadmap reference: T2406.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/windows/qt_source_clip_preview_model.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_retarget_window.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/qt_lib.py`, `tests/test_retarget_external_import.py`, `tests/test_retarget_workbench_ui_placement.py`.

- Wired the production UE/FBX source-animation import backend into the Retarget Workbench. `import_ue_fbx_animation_clip()` now defaults to a headless Blender bridge that imports binary FBX files, evaluates armature pose matrices through Blender, converts the extracted payload into the existing `SourceSkeletonClip` contract, preserves parent hierarchy and rest matrices, and records Blender axis/unit conversion metadata. This reuses the MCP/automation extraction path instead of maintaining a separate GUI-only importer. Roadmap reference: T2405.

- Fixed the Animation Retargeting Workbench external FBX import route so selecting a UE/FBX source clip no longer trips the legacy `core` package import path in the generic mesh converter. `mesh_converter` now imports KOTOR model structures from the canonical `src.core.geometry.model_data` module, and the workbench routes Unreal->KOTOR source `.fbx` files directly to the source-clip importer before attempting generic mesh conversion. KOTOR->Unreal target `.fbx` files likewise route to the Unreal skeleton importer first, giving an actionable backend message instead of a misleading mesh-import failure. Roadmap reference: T2405.

- Fixed the reverse-retarget animation bake so mapped UE/source world-space bone motion is preserved as Aurora local orientation controller motion instead of being flattened when source parent and child tracks move together. Added regressions for the parent-child cancellation case and for rejecting a flattened arm export. Rebuilt and installed a new custom-patch PMBAM `kpmwin1` candidate after the amplitude gate passed. - Fixed the animation-only MDL injection writer so appended local animation blocks serialize animation nodes in vanilla depth-first order: node header, child pointer array, child subtrees, then controller data. The raw export gate now simulates the `UpdateAnimFootprint` child traversal by reading child count at `+0x30`, child pointer array at `+0x2c`, validating child node offsets/cycles, checking declared traversal count for new exports, and requiring child subtrees to start immediately after the child array. Added a regression comparing the new PMBAM `kpmwin1` block against stock `S_Female02.f2a2` serialization order. Roadmap reference: T2402 export safety.

- Added the read-only `GameResourceProvider` foundation so Character, Retarget, Module, Map, and future MCP/Qt resource surfaces can resolve KOTOR resources through one `ResourceAddress`-backed interface instead of per-studio ad hoc lookup. - Added the initial `KOTOR → Unreal` Retarget Workbench pipeline. The new mode samples a KOTOR/Aurora source animation through the existing sampler, retargets it onto an Unreal target skeleton contract, bakes a UE-compatible animation clip with in-place root policy and rest-preserved twist/IK/helper bones, and exports the exact preview through ExportJob using an FBX backend interface. When no backend is configured, export fails before writing with an actionable backend message. The Qt workbench now exposes a UE output clip-name field and treats all three retarget directions as product flows. Roadmap reference: T2404/T2405.

- Added a third-pass coding-book scope sanity report and current-state audit for GhostRigger. The new report re-anchors the product around four studios, clarifies what GhostRigger is for, what should stay out of scope, and adds a mandatory scope test for future features: owning studio, modder task, target resource/object, capability label, validation/export gates, and remaining incompleteness. The scope audit gives an honest assessment that Retarget Studio is currently the strongest product surface, shared architecture foundations are real but not yet fully integrated, Character Studio native KOTOR DAG export is the highest product risk, and Module/Map Studio need provider-backed resources, undo, and ExportJob migration before broad modder-facing editing. Roadmap reference: T2304/T2305 planning.
  Affected areas: `knowledge_base/book_notes/coding_books_third_pass_scope_sanity_2026_05_23.md`, `docs/audits/ghostrigger_scope_sanity_audit_2026_05_23.md`, `docs/audits/ghostrigger_program_architecture_audit_2026_05_23.md`, `docs/audits/ghostrigger_roadmap_adjustments_2026_05_23.md`, `knowledge_base/roadmap/04_full_suite_completion_roadmap_20260522.md`, `knowledge_base/book_notes/ghostrigger_programming_crosswalk.md`, `knowledge_base/book_notes/README.md`, `knowledge_base/README.md`.

- Added a coding-focused GhostRigger knowledge-base refresh and current architecture sanity audit. The new notes synthesize Qt/PySide6 GUI books, official Qt documentation, Refactoring UI, Architecture Patterns with Python, and Clean Architecture into GhostRigger-specific rules for Qt actions, model/view, undoable authoring, background jobs, service boundaries, ResourceAddress, ValidationBus, ExportJob, and modder-facing capability honesty. A second pass across all six books added missed guidance around proxy models, data mappers, filesystem models, QSettings boundaries, QProcess/service bridges, progress/cancel jobs, empty states, defaults, and undo-before-authoring. A verified KOTOR modding source hierarchy was also added so future work can distinguish implementation truth, mature community tools, and tutorial/workflow references. The audit updates the current program snapshot and recommends the next roadmap order: `GameResourceProvider`, provider-backed resource models, ValidationBus issue panel, Module/Map undo foundation, shared job/progress bridge, Retarget quality mode polish, and Character Studio native DAG lock. Roadmap reference: T2304/T2305 planning.

## 2026-05-22

- Added a modder-facing Retarget Workbench readiness/status layer. The new readiness model summarizes the active mode, selected source, target, target output animation, vanilla-slot versus custom-patch runtime behavior, missing inputs, preview readiness, export readiness, and stale-preview reasons without invoking samplers, solvers, writers, or ExportJob. The Qt shell now exposes stable readiness labels near the mode selector so modders can see whether an output is vanilla-safe or custom-patch-only before preview/export. Roadmap reference: T2405.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/retarget_workbench_readiness.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_retarget_workbench_controller.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `tests/test_retarget_workbench_readiness.py`, `.gitignore`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_retarget_workbench_readiness.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_workbench_readiness`; `python -m pytest tests/test_ghostrigger_retarget_workbench_controller.py tests/test_ghostrigger_retarget_output_naming_controller.py tests/test_ghostrigger_kotor_to_kotor_workbench_controller.py tests/test_retarget_workbench_readiness.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_workbench_ux`; `python -m pytest tests -q -k "retarget or project or validation or export" --maxfail=10 --disable-warnings --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_workbench_ux_targeted`; `python -m py_compile src/core/retargeting/retarget_workbench_readiness.py src/core/retargeting/retarget_modes.py src/core/retargeting/retarget_output_naming.py src/gui/windows/qt_retarget_workbench_controller.py src/gui/windows/qt_retarget_preview_controller.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_retarget_workbench_readiness.py`.

- Enabled the Retarget Workbench `KOTOR → KOTOR` mode. The new adapter samples a source KOTOR/Odyssey animation slot through the existing Aurora evaluator/supermodel resolver into a `SourceSkeletonClip`, then reuses the verified preview pipeline to retarget onto a target KOTOR model as either a vanilla slot override or custom animation patch name. The workbench now treats `KOTOR → KOTOR` as an implemented preview/export mode, stores the sampled source and last preview result, applies previews through the existing in-memory viewport adapter, and exports only the exact last preview through the existing ExportJob/Aurora writer path. Roadmap reference: T2403.

- Added custom KOTOR output animation naming for the Retarget Workbench. KOTOR-targeted retarget output can now stay in strict vanilla-slot mode or opt into custom-patch mode, where the generated `AnimationBlock.name` preserves a validated custom name exactly and marks preview/export metadata as requiring the custom animation patch/runtime. Vanilla slot resolution remains the default, display labels never become animation names, preview/export state invalidates when output naming changes, and the Qt shell now exposes vanilla/custom naming controls plus slot/custom-name fields. - Added the shared `ExportJob` transaction foundation for safe multi-artifact exports. The new helper performs preflight validation, same-directory staging, writer callbacks against staged paths, verifier callbacks before promotion, manifest promotion support, best-effort rollback/cleanup, and optional `ValidationBus` publication. Retarget preview MDL/MDX export now uses this staged transaction path while still exporting the exact approved preview animation block through the existing Aurora writer/readback gates. The new validation package defines UI/navigation-friendly `ValidationIssue`, `ValidationReport`, `ValidationSeverity`, `ValidationSubsystem`, and `ValidationNavigationTarget` models with ResourceAddress-backed targets, JSON roundtrip helpers, deterministic issue IDs, source replacement/append behavior, subscriber snapshots, report merging, and adapters for project validation plus retarget preview audits. - Fixed the retarget MDL export path so animation-only PMBAM overrides no longer rebuild or compact the vanilla mesh/skin payload. `AuroraAnimationWriter` now routes local animation overrides through an MDL writer injection path that copies the source MDL geometry/name/node/mesh bytes and source MDX byte-for-byte, patches only the model file size plus local animation table fields, and appends the new animation block. Added PMBAM regression coverage proving the exported MDX is identical to vanilla and that the original MDL prefix differs only in those animation/header bytes, which protects skinned-node bonemaps, qbones/tbones, skin weights, mesh index tables, bounds, render/shadow flags, and texture casing from retarget export drift. Roadmap reference: T2402 export safety.

- Fixed the MDL binary writer's final name-table casing regression after live K1 testing showed exported PMBAM still entered A-pose even with full controller coverage. The writer now preserves Aurora node names exactly as loaded from the target model instead of lowercasing the geometry and animation name table, and the animation export-tree validator now compares node names, parents, and child lists case-sensitively. Added PMBAM regression coverage that injects `victory`, reloads the exported MDL/MDX, compares geometry node names/parent paths against vanilla PMBAM byte-for-byte/case-for-case, verifies the local `victory` animation node names use the same casing, and confirms inherited `pause1`, `walk`, and `run` still resolve through the supermodel chain. Roadmap reference: T2402 export safety.
  Affected areas: `src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py`, `tests/test_aurora_animation_writer.py`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_aurora_animation_writer.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_case_preserve`; `python -m pytest tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py tests/test_retarget_preview_export.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_case_export_gates`; `python -m py_compile src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py src/core/retargeting/aurora_animation_writer.py src/core/game/kotor_loader.py`.

- Fixed the reverse-retarget MDL export path after K1 live testing showed `victory` loaded but evaluated in A-pose because only 14 limb nodes carried controllers. R3.A/R3.B animation generation now emits full-hierarchy orientation controllers for every target Aurora node, including constant rest-pose keys for unmoving core/control branches such as `rootdummy`, `pelvis_g`, `torso_g`, `torsoUpr_g`, `rcollar_g`, and `lcollar_g`. Root/pelvis position controllers are emitted when source root motion is detected, and the R3.B size gate now allows the expected growth from full-sampled controller coverage. The binary MDL writer also defensively backfills rest-pose orientation controllers on otherwise controllerless animation tree nodes so sparse callers cannot produce a valid-but-unposed local override. Roadmap reference: T2402 export safety.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/aurora_animation_writer.py`, `src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py`, `tests/test_aurora_animation_writer.py`.

- Locked AgentDecompile/Ghidra adapter configuration to local/env-only defaults so private hosts, usernames, and passwords are not exposed through the GitHub repo. The adapter no longer hardcodes the live Ghidra MCP IP or default shared-server username, `.env` and local MCP/Ghidra config patterns are ignored, and a tracked-file hygiene test now fails if the known private endpoint/password values are committed. - Fixed the MDL animation export tree shape after K1 live testing showed the corrected model header still crashed in `UpdateAnimFootprint` before KPM playback hooks could run. Exported local animation blocks now clone the full target Aurora hierarchy and overlay controllers onto matching nodes by name, so unkeyed toes, hands, fingers, helper nodes, and mesh placeholders remain present with valid parent/child arrays. The main writer and legacy porter now validate that the binary animation export tree mirrors the target node order, parents, and child lists before writing. Added raw-byte regression coverage that walks the emitted animation child arrays and fails if sparse animation blocks omit unkeyed target nodes. Roadmap reference: T2402 export safety.
  Affected areas: `src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py`, `src/core/mdl/mdl_porter.py`, `tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py`, `knowledge_base/reference/ghidra_odyssey_mcp.md`.
  Verification: Ghidra MCP confirmed `UpdateAnimFootprint` walks animation node child arrays and reads child node fields at `0x00437f3f`; `python -m pytest tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_anim_tree`; `python -m pytest tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_anim_tree_export`; rebuilt and installed the local K1 Override `pmbam.mdl/.mdx` with `.animtree_20260522T083000.bak` backups, then verified live header fields, `victory` animation subtype `0x05`, raw animation `node_count=61`, child-array walk `61/61` with no invalid offsets, and GhostRigger readback `PMBAM` with local animation `victory` containing 61 nodes.

- Fixed the MDL binary writers' full engine model header layout after K1 live testing showed the subtype-byte fix was necessary but not sufficient: exported retarget candidates still crashed in `InputBinary::Read` because GhostRigger emitted an 88-byte model-field payload where PyKotor/the engine expect `_ModelHeader.SIZE = 0xC4` / 196 bytes. The main writer and legacy porter now emit the full post-geometry model fields, patch `offset_to_super_root`, preserve `mdx_data_buffer_offset`, `mdx_size`, `mdx_offset`, and place the name-offset table at the canonical `0xC4` boundary with duplicate counts. Added raw-byte regression coverage and expanded the Odyssey Ghidra MCP knowledge note with the model-header offsets. Roadmap reference: T2402 export safety.
  Affected areas: `src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py`, `src/core/mdl/mdl_porter.py`, `tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py`, `knowledge_base/reference/ghidra_odyssey_mcp.md`.
  Verification: Ghidra MCP reconfirmed the crash path in `InputBinary::Read` / `MaxTree::AsModel`; compared stock PMBAM header fields against the generated Override candidate; `python -m pytest tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_model_header_gate`; `python -m py_compile src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py src/core/mdl/mdl_porter.py tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py`; rebuilt and installed the local K1 Override `pmbam.mdl/.mdx` with timestamped `.headerfix_20260522T075425.bak` backups, then verified live header fields `topSubtype=0x02`, `offsetToSuperRoot=1028`, `mdxSize=148960`, `nameOffsets=196`, `animSubtype=0x05`, and GhostRigger/PyKotor readback `PMBAM` with local animation `victory`.

- Fixed the MDL binary writer's engine-facing MaxTree subtype bytes after K1 live testing showed exported retarget candidates could crash `swkotor.exe` in `InputBinary::Read` when `MaxTree::AsModel` returned null. The main writer now emits `0x02` at top-level geometry header `+0x4C` and `0x05` at every animation geometry header `+0x4C`; the legacy porter path now writes the top-level model subtype at the same engine offset instead of one byte late. Added raw-byte regression coverage for the emitted headers and documented the Odyssey Ghidra MCP workflow without storing credentials. Roadmap reference: T2402/T2403 export safety.
  Affected areas: `src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py`, `src/core/mdl/mdl_porter.py`, `tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py`, `knowledge_base/reference/ghidra_odyssey_mcp.md`, `knowledge_base/README.md`.
  Verification: Ghidra MCP confirmed `MaxTree::AsModel` reads byte `[ECX+0x4C]`, masks with `0x7F`, and requires model subtype `0x02` before `InputBinary::Read` writes through the returned pointer; `python -m pytest tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_mdl_subtype_gate`; patched the live K1 Override `pmbam.mdl` after backing it up to `pmbam.mdl.bak`, with the only byte differences at `0x58: 00→02` and `0x29814: 00→05`; GhostRigger/PyKotor readback loads `PMBAM` with local animation `victory`.

- Added the KOTOR/Odyssey animation source sampler for future Retarget Workbench directions. The workbench pending-mode status now records that the KOTOR source sampler is available while `KOTOR → KOTOR` preview and `KOTOR → Unreal` FBX export adapters remain pending. - Refined the PMBAM UE idle retarget path after viewport testing showed that full source-rest transfer over-bent the body while clip-frame-zero transfer erased the authored idle pose. R3.B now defaults to a hybrid reference policy that keeps root/torso/lower-body channels stable against clip frame 0 while blending shoulder/arm/finger bind-pose deltas by a tunable weight, and the injection CLI exposes `--source-reference-mode` plus `--hybrid-limb-source-rest-weight` for future calibration passes. The UE-to-Aurora converter now uses the observed PMBAM/UE rest-pose convention `(-X, -Y, Z)`, fixing the old unmirrored-X/Y basis that left arms in an A-pose. The PMBAM rename map now targets real forearm nodes (`Lforearm_g`/`Rforearm_g`) instead of the intermediate bicep links and includes two-joint finger mappings. The final scratch candidate used calibrated-basis exact segment correction across arms, hands, fingers, thighs, shins, feet, and toes; its durable findings now live in the retargeting verification knowledge base while generated MDL/MDX outputs stay ignored. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.

- Added a 3D Math Primer research note for the GhostRigger retargeting pipeline. The new knowledgebase entry fills the practical math gaps around multiple coordinate spaces, nested parent-local spaces, row/column convention hazards, orthonormal basis construction, quaternion negation/inverse/multiplication/difference, plane/pole diagnostics, skeletal bind-pose implications, and why direction matching alone cannot solve PMBAM arm/hand pose quality. - No roadmap task ID applies: Added the Autodesk FBX SDK Setup Assistant for local, user-managed SDK configuration without bundling Autodesk files. The assistant detects Python/platform/architecture, opens Autodesk's official FBX SDK download page, lets users select SDK/binding/FbxCommon paths, scans likely SDK files, tests `import fbx`, optional `import FbxCommon`, SDK manager creation, and empty scene creation, then stores verified path settings under `settings.json` `fbx_sdk`. FBX import/export now applies configured SDK paths and prompts to open setup or Autodesk's download page when the SDK is missing. Added setup documentation, an environment diagnostic script, requirements comments, and `.gitignore` guards for local SDK folders. Affected areas: `src/io/fbx/`, `src/gui/dialogs/fbx_sdk_setup_dialog.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/qt_lib.py`, `scripts/print_fbx_python_environment.py`, `docs/FBX_SDK_SETUP.md`, `.gitignore`, `requirements.txt`, Qt import tests. Verification: `python -m py_compile src/io/fbx/fbx_sdk_paths.py src/io/fbx/fbx_sdk_setup.py src/io/fbx/fbx_sdk_loader.py src/io/fbx/fbx_diagnostics.py src/io/fbx/fbx_importer.py src/io/fbx/fbx_exporter.py src/gui/dialogs/fbx_sdk_setup_dialog.py src/gui/qt_lib.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py scripts/print_fbx_python_environment.py tests/test_qt_only_imports.py`; `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py -q`; `python scripts/print_fbx_python_environment.py` confirmed this Python 3.13 environment has no `fbx`/`FbxCommon` and reports that cleanly.
  Follow-up: FBX import now auto-detects the recommended backend before import begins and presents that decision as an explicit `Use Auto` choice alongside manual Autodesk SDK and Blender FBX choices. Auto-detection checks the configured Autodesk SDK first, then Blender availability, and the selected backend is used without silently falling through to the other backend after import starts. Roadmap reference: T2408.

- No roadmap task ID applies: Added an optional Autodesk Python FBX SDK bridge for static mesh FBX import/export. The new bridge safely detects `fbx`/`FbxCommon`, imports FBX hierarchy/meshes/transforms/materials/UVs/normals into `KotorModel` objects that enter the existing KMAX scene/viewport path, exports current or selected scene meshes through Autodesk SDK when installed, and adds FBX SDK diagnostics plus clear missing-SDK warnings in the Qt UI. Affected areas: `src/io/fbx/`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `requirements.txt`, Qt import/FBX bridge tests. Verification: `python -m py_compile src/io/__init__.py src/io/fbx/__init__.py src/io/fbx/fbx_sdk_loader.py src/io/fbx/fbx_scene_adapter.py src/io/fbx/fbx_importer.py src/io/fbx/fbx_exporter.py src/io/fbx/fbx_diagnostics.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_qt_only_imports.py`; `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py -q`; direct FBX diagnostic command confirmed this Python 3.13 environment lacks `fbx` and `FbxCommon` and reports the missing dependency without crashing.

## 2026-05-22

- T1403: Added the first unified Qt Content Browser pass for Library and Animation Library workflows. The main shell now routes model-library scan/load/extract/batch/add-to-scene/retarget actions and current-model animation load/preview/export actions through a dockable Content Browser with asset navigation, searchable/filterable metadata, details/preview affordances, context actions, and first-class animation rows. Content Browser, Scene, Properties, and Animations are now independent dock widgets around a central viewport instead of fixed side tabs, freeing the viewport as the main workspace. Added the `contentBrowser` and `scene` layout panel ids while keeping legacy `library` and `animationLibrary` layout ids for compatibility. Affected areas: `src/gui/panels/qt_content_browser_panel.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/qt_lib.py`, `src/gui/libtheme/`, `config/themes/layouts/`, theme/layout docs, `.gitignore`, tests. - MDL parser stock-corpus recovery: Patched GhostRigger's owned binary MDL reader to apply logical MDL bounds when reading variable-length trimesh, child-node, and light flare arrays, and to sanitize non-finite face plane coefficients/normals before PyKotor converts faces. This fixes the 54 stock model parse failures captured in `diagnostics/mdl_parser_errors_2026-05-22.json`, including `K1:fx_explode_03`, `K2:504ondh`, and `K1:w_dblsbr_001`. No roadmap task ID applies. Affected areas: `src/core/mdl/ghostrigger_mdl_reader.py`, `tests/test_regression.py`. Verification: MCP helper ground-truth checks confirmed the three representative failures now load through `inspect_mdl`, `inspect_mdl_ghostrigger`, and `compare_model_pipelines`; prior-failure replay checked 54/54 models with 0 remaining errors; full manifest parser sweep checked 6,069 models with 0 parser errors and saved `diagnostics/mdl_parser_errors_after_patch_2026-05-22.json`.
- Main UI Tk-removal cleanup and MDL parser diagnostics: Removed the dead `Open Legacy Tk Workbench` action from the Qt Tools menu and deleted the `_launch_legacy_tk` process launcher, keeping the main shell aligned with M3/T302's completed Tk removal. ## 2026-05-21

- Added a Game Engine Architecture research note for the GhostRigger retargeting pipeline. The new knowledgebase entry records useful Volume I lessons for treating retargeting as an asset-conditioning pipeline, keeping solver/evaluator/viewport/writer subsystem boundaries separate, preserving KOTOR MDL/MDX resource identity, making clip timing deterministic, and expanding debug overlay/capture/report tooling before Override testing. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `knowledge_base/retargeting/research/game_engine_architecture_for_aurora_retargeting.md`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/README.md`.
  Verification: local PDF outline and keyword/page scan; documentation-only update; `git diff --check`.

- Added a book-backed calibrated retarget-frame layer for Aurora node animation experiments. The new research note records the John Vince graphics-math principles now guiding the retargeter, especially basis vectors, inverse/order-sensitive transforms, orthonormal frames, quaternion composition, and interpolation. The new opt-in `calibrated_frame_delta` solver path builds full source/target segment frames from mapped reference poses, transfers current source frame motion through those calibrated bases, and includes synthetic coverage for PMBAM-style intermediate arm chains without changing the default safer `reference_frame_delta` mode. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/retarget_calibration.py`, `src/core/retargeting/retarget_solver.py`, `tests/test_retarget_calibration.py`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/research/computer_graphics_math_for_aurora_retargeting.md`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/README.md`.

- Added the in-memory retarget viewport preview gate. GhostRigger can now build a preview model from the pure solver result, attach the generated Aurora animation as a local override on a deep-copied target model, keep the original target untouched, run a headless numeric audit over evaluator playback, block preview application on invalid slots/unknown nodes/non-finite transforms/non-root translation drift/root drift, and apply successful previews through a small viewport adapter with optional node overlay and standard angle capture hooks. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/retarget_preview.py`, `tests/test_retarget_preview_gate.py`, `.gitignore`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_retarget_preview_gate.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_preview`; `python -m pytest tests/test_animation_slot_resolution.py tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py tests/test_aurora_animation_controller_semantics.py tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py tests/test_ue_fbx_source_import.py tests/test_retarget_mapping_profile.py tests/test_retarget_reference_pose.py tests/test_basic_retarget_solver.py tests/test_retarget_preview_gate.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_gates`; `python -m py_compile src/core/retargeting/source_animation.py src/core/retargeting/retarget_profile.py src/core/retargeting/retarget_mapping.py src/core/retargeting/reference_pose.py src/core/retargeting/retarget_solver.py src/core/retargeting/retarget_preview.py src/core/animation/animation_engine.py src/core/game/kotor_loader.py src/core/geometry/model_data.py src/core/validation/animation_block_validator.py`; `git diff --check`.

- Added the first basic UE-source to KOTOR/Aurora retarget solver. The new pure solver validates the retarget profile and KOTOR slot, builds explicit source/target reference poses, transfers sampled source global rotation motion into mapped target reference frames, emits absolute parent-relative Aurora orientation controllers only for mapped existing nodes, strips root translation by default, leaves non-root translations at target reference/rest values, preserves quaternion hemisphere continuity, validates the generated animation block, and reports solve diagnostics without mutating source clips, profiles, or target models. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/retarget_solver.py`, `src/core/retargeting/retarget_frames.py`, `src/core/retargeting/root_motion.py`, `src/core/retargeting/retarget_solve_audit.py`, `tests/test_basic_retarget_solver.py`, `.gitignore`.

- Added the source-to-KOTOR retarget mapping profile and reference-pose gate. GhostRigger can now load/save normalized retarget profiles, suggest source/target semantic roles without trusting them blindly, reject unknown UE source nodes, unknown Aurora target nodes, duplicate normal target mappings, helper/twist/IK misuse, and invalid KOTOR animation slots. The new reference builder constructs explicit source clip-rest/clip-time and target rest/animation-slot poses without mutating assets, and the mapped segment audit reports source/target lengths, direction angles, ratios, and zero-length warnings for later retarget-frame construction. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.

- Added a backend-neutral UE/FBX source animation import and sampled-pose gate. The new source layer represents imported skeleton clips as parent-before-child source nodes, rest/reference poses, sampled global/local transforms, explicit axis/unit metadata, twist/IK/helper/root/deform classifications, matrix-based basis conversion helpers, and a source skeleton audit report. The public FBX importer now fails clearly when no supported backend is configured, while deterministic fake-backend tests prove evaluated global matrix sampling, local reconstruction, frame-0 reference fallback warnings, multi-clip selection behavior, and optional real-FBX fixture gating. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/source_animation.py`, `src/core/retargeting/fbx_importer.py`, `src/core/retargeting/coordinate.py`, `src/core/retargeting/source_skeleton_audit.py`, `tests/test_ue_fbx_source_import.py`, `.gitignore`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_ue_fbx_source_import.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_ue_fbx_import`; `python -m pytest tests/test_animation_slot_resolution.py tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py tests/test_aurora_animation_controller_semantics.py tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py tests/test_ue_fbx_source_import.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_gates`; `python -m py_compile src/core/retargeting/source_animation.py src/core/retargeting/fbx_importer.py src/core/retargeting/coordinate.py src/core/retargeting/source_skeleton_audit.py src/core/geometry/model_data.py`.

- Added an MDL animation override writer/readback round-trip gate. Export requests can now opt into post-write verification that reloads the written MDL/MDX, confirms the local override exists under the canonical KOTOR slot name, compares controller times and values with quaternion sign-equivalence, evaluates pre-write and read-back poses through the Aurora semantics oracle, checks node hierarchy/rest-transform preservation, and reports writer-boundary mismatches before a bad export can be handed off. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `src/core/validation/animation_roundtrip_validator.py`, `src/core/retargeting/aurora_animation_writer.py`, `tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py`, `.gitignore`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_mdl_anim_roundtrip`; `python -m pytest tests/test_animation_slot_resolution.py tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py tests/test_aurora_animation_controller_semantics.py tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py tests/test_mdl_animation_override_roundtrip.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_gates`; `python -m py_compile src/core/animation/animation_engine.py src/core/geometry/model_data.py src/core/game/kotor_loader.py src/core/retargeting/aurora_animation_writer.py src/core/validation/animation_block_validator.py src/core/validation/animation_roundtrip_validator.py src/core/mdl/mdl_writer.py`.

- Added a synthetic Aurora animation controller semantics gate. The new headless evaluator treats orientation and position controller keys as absolute parent-local values, falls back to rest transforms for unkeyed components, propagates parent transforms by FK, and verifies shortest-path quaternion interpolation. Export-ready animation injection now also runs structural animation-block validation after slot resolution and before local override mutation or MDL/MDX writing, rejecting unknown KOTOR nodes and invalid key data without creating output files. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `src/core/animation/animation_engine.py`, `src/core/validation/animation_block_validator.py`, `src/core/retargeting/aurora_animation_writer.py`, `tests/test_aurora_animation_controller_semantics.py`, `.gitignore`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_aurora_animation_controller_semantics.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_controller_semantics`; `python -m pytest tests/test_animation_slot_resolution.py tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py tests/test_aurora_animation_controller_semantics.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_gates`; `python -m py_compile src/core/animation/animation_engine.py src/core/geometry/model_data.py src/core/game/kotor_loader.py src/core/retargeting/aurora_animation_writer.py src/core/validation/animation_block_validator.py`.

- Wired strict KOTOR animation slot validation into the Aurora animation override export boundary. Export-ready MDL injection now resolves the requested slot through the target model/supermodel chain before local override mutation or MDL/MDX writing, copies the outgoing animation to the canonical resolved slot name, rejects UE clip names that are not valid KOTOR slots, and returns before creating export files on invalid slots. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/aurora_animation_writer.py`, `tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py`, `.gitignore`.
  Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_animation_export_slot_gate.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_slot_export_gate`; `python -m pytest tests/test_animation_slot_resolution.py tests/test_roundtrip_verification.py -q --basetemp .pytest_tmp_retarget_gate`.

- Added KOTOR animation slot and supermodel-chain resolver helpers for the GhostRigger UE-to-Aurora retargeting workflow. The loader now exposes local-first valid slot listing, inherited-slot resolution with local override metadata, and diagnostic supermodel chain metadata so retarget exports can verify a real KOTOR slot before writing an MDL/MDX override. Roadmap reference: T1403 animation playback workflow.
