- Restored the grouped `src.core.diagnostics` compatibility package after syncing LordVader's latest `qt-ghostrigger` reorganization, so `src.core.qt_core` can expose validation and module-reference helpers alongside the moved `animation`, `geometry`, `game`, and `mdl` packages. No roadmap task ID applies; this is repository-sync compatibility work.
  Affected areas: `src/core/diagnostics/`, `src/core/qt_core.py` import surface.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile scripts\verify_roundtrip.py src\core\mdl\mdl_writer.py tests\test_roundtrip_verification.py src\core\validation\viewport_validator.py src\core\retargeting\aurora_animation_writer.py`; focused round-trip/core contract pytest after the sync.

- Added theme-specific Matrix bar colours and a dedicated `matrix` font role. All packaged themes now define `matrixBar.*` colour tokens matched to their palette, and the Matrix bar font defaults to the bundled `Aurebesh AF` font while remaining editable in the Theme Editor Fonts tab. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Follow-up: gave the header IPC status label its own `HeaderIpcMeta` object name and verified it resolves through `matrixBar.ipcText` for every packaged theme instead of inheriting the generic header meta colour.
  Follow-up: fixed the crash reported in `logs/ghostrigger_2026-05-21_135423.log`; the Matrix bar painter now passes `QtGui.QFont.Weight` enum values to `QFont.setWeight()` instead of raw integers, which PySide6 rejected during repaint.
  Follow-up: compacted the Settings dialog by splitting it into top-level tabs for Paths, General, Matrix Bar, Theme/Layout, and Measurement, with scrollable tab pages and two-row action grids so hidden sections no longer force a huge window size.
  Follow-up: fixed Settings dialog theme application so Apply Theme targets and repaints the dialog itself instead of disabling the parent main window updates, preventing the settings window from visually disappearing until resize.
  Follow-up: polished the Theme Editor metric tables. Theme and layout metrics now hide row numbers, use bold label widgets inside metric cells, use spin-box value editors with explicit unit suffixes, and include tab width/padding/margin metrics in both fallback tokens and packaged layout XML. Layout application and preview now understand the new tab sizing tokens.
  Follow-up: lowered the default tab height to 14 px and added theme-controlled tab style modes. Themes now load/save `<styles><style name="tab.mode" value="standard|flat|beveled"/></styles>`, the Theme Editor exposes a tab style mode selector, and the stylesheet builder renders standard, flat, and beveled tab treatments.
  Follow-up: refined collapsible tool sections. The shared `CollapsibleGroupBox` expander is now a fixed square control that ignores global button minimum sizes, reserves stable header space, restores expanded geometry, and collapses to a fixed header height. Mesh Tools now top-aligns sections with stretch only after the final group so collapsed sections stack from top to bottom.
  Follow-up: made Settings dialog Apply Theme persist immediately by emitting the normal settings payload after a successful apply, so the chosen theme is written to `settings.json` and no longer reverts to the previously saved Classic selection on restart.
  Follow-up: made Theme Editor Apply Theme persist through the main window settings writer as well. Theme/layout settings now merge with theme-owned fields applied after layout-owned fields so stale layout-manager data cannot overwrite the selected theme.
  Follow-up: moved theme stylesheet generation onto a worker `QThread` and added theme-apply progress signals. The main window now shows the themed progress toast during theme application, updates it while the background stylesheet build runs, and keeps it visible before the unavoidable main-thread Qt widget restyle step.
  Follow-up: moved the viewport mode toolbar out of the viewport widget and into a shell-owned toolbar band below the main command bar. The same viewport buttons are reparented into the new band, while side spacers track the left and right panel widths so the strip sits in the central space above the viewport column.
  Follow-up: changed the Output Log title from a clickable tool button into a plain themed section label inside the log pane, matching the Python terminal presentation. Both Output Log and Python section labels now use stylesheet tokens for monospace title styling.
  Follow-up: added the startup-console look as a packaged `Droid` theme. The new `config/themes/themes/droid.xml` uses dark graphite panels, grey controls, high-contrast text, Matrix-green accents, matching Matrix bar tokens, and the bundled Aurebesh Matrix font. Docs and theme-loading tests now list Droid as a built-in default theme.
  Follow-up: moved the header, command bar, and viewport toolbar into a non-movable reserved top `QToolBar` so the viewport tool strip sits directly under Open/Auto-Rig/Character Builder and Qt docks cannot occupy the top UI area. Detachable/toolbox docks now explicitly disallow the top dock area and are redirected back to workspace side areas if needed.
  Follow-up: replaced the reserved top area's horizontal scroll strips with wrapping flow layouts. At the default window width, command buttons and viewport controls now wrap onto left-aligned rows underneath instead of clipping or requiring a horizontal scrollbar; layout application no longer forces those wrapping rows back to a single fixed height.
  Follow-up: added a built-in `Default` native theme. `config/themes/themes/default.xml` sets `application.native=true`, causing the stylesheet builder to return no QSS and the theme applier to restore the platform Qt palette instead of applying GhostRigger font/palette overrides. Applying Default clears local GhostRigger styles and disables the Matrix bar animation so the app can be inspected with plain platform styling.
  Follow-up: fixed the gimbal crash caused by a renderer instance missing `show_gimbal`. The viewport now initializes that compatibility flag, all gimbal hover/toggle/draw paths use a defensive accessor, and the CPU base-render suppression path preserves/restores the visible gimbal state instead of converting a missing attribute into "gimbal off."
  Follow-up: restored the visible model transform gimbal after the compatibility fix. The viewport now falls back to the loaded model root if selection state is dropped, positions the root gimbal at the model bounds center like the previous renderer path, and feeds the overlay gizmo from the active theme gimbal colours.
  Follow-up: fixed the Default native theme viewport regression where the advanced transform gizmo, corner axes gimbal, and HUD stats disappeared. `FrameRenderer` now initializes render/overlay runtime state before any theme hook runs, theme colour application no longer resets animation/gimbal/walkmesh state, and native theme application resets viewport/GPU colours without requiring QSS.
  Follow-up: tied the native Default look into the viewport renderer itself. Default/native mode now derives the CPU canvas, HUD pills, grid lines, gimbal highlight text, GPU clear colour, GPU grid colours, and GPU wire colour from the active Qt palette instead of falling back to GhostRigger's dark renderer palette.
  Follow-up: restored native-style contrast for themed spin-box steppers. All custom themes now define editable `spinbox.*` colour tokens for the up/down button fill, hover, pressed, border, and arrow colours, and the generated QSS draws bordered stepper containers for `QSpinBox` and `QDoubleSpinBox` instead of leaving them visually flat against the field.
  Follow-up: replaced the QSS triangle arrows with explicit light/dark SVG arrow assets for spin-box steppers, fixing the themed up/down controls that were rendering square blocks instead of arrows.
  Follow-up: added startup theme stylesheet precaching before the Qt main-window launch. `ThemeApplier` now shares a process-wide stylesheet cache, and `main.py` prints per-theme precache progress plus a summary to the launching terminal so later theme switches use warmed stylesheets immediately.
  Follow-up: fixed collapsible tool-section expanders so they stay small square controls instead of inheriting global command-button sizing. The shared `CollapsibleGroupBox` toggle now opts out of layout button-mode sizing and the generated theme QSS includes a dedicated fixed-size selector for `QToolButton#CollapsibleGroupToggle`.
  Follow-up: moved Matrix bar controls out of Settings and into the Theme Editor. Matrix bar mode, glyphs, font override, and PNG/GIF path are now theme `<styles>` entries (`matrixBar.style`, `matrixBar.glyphs`, `matrixBar.fontFamily`, `matrixBar.imagePath`), while older app-level Matrix bar settings remain a fallback for existing user configs.
  Follow-up: fixed Default/native theme Matrix bar images. Native theme application now honors the active theme's Matrix bar config instead of forcing Matrix panels disabled, and the Theme Editor Matrix Bar preview renders selected PNG/GIF media instead of always showing the glyph strip.
  Follow-up: limited Matrix bar media to the top header only. The command bar is a normal themed frame again, Matrix media rendering now supports percentage crop rectangles, and the Theme Editor preview includes a bounded, scaled image selector so large source images no longer force the editor window oversized.
  Follow-up: adjusted the Matrix Bar image preview into a fixed-height source-image box with a crop rectangle overlay. The preview displays the full source image scaled into the box, the crop rectangle defines the Matrix bar slice, and the media no longer resizes the Theme Editor window.

## 2026-05-20

- Upgraded the Qt lightmap baker into a UV-aware, previewable Python-native baking workflow. Added artist-facing UV channel inspection (`UV1`/`UV2`/`UV3` mapped to zero-indexed internals), stricter validation for overlaps/out-of-bounds/coverage, xatlas-backed lightmap UV generation with split UV index storage and a safe fallback atlas, Open3D raycasting support with CPU fallback, preview caching, preview/comparison windows, live preview worker cancellation, and final bake assignment metadata that does not overwrite original MDL lightmap fields. No roadmap task ID applies.

- Added Sprint 1 Day 4 FBX export for the locked `pmbam + g1a1 + aligned Quinn` forward path. GhostRigger now serializes a deterministic intermediate JSON, invokes Blender 4.2 LTS in background mode, exports a single self-contained FBX with mesh + aligned skeleton + baked animation, re-imports the FBX through Blender for roundtrip validation, writes a manifest sidecar, and exposes the narrow Day 4 path through `ghostrigger_export_unity_fbx`.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/fbx_exporter.py`, `scripts/blender_fbx_export.py`, `src/core/retargeting/__init__.py`, `src/kotormcp/tools/retargeting.py`, `src/kotormcp/tools/__init__.py`, `tests/test_fbx_exporter.py`, `tests/test_mcp_retargeting_tools.py`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/ground_truth/day4_20260521_000000Z/g1a1_metadata.json`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/audits/fbx_exporter_day4_20260520.md`, `exports/retargets/day4/`.
  Verification: Blender 4.2.0 was detected; `python -m pytest tests\test_fbx_exporter.py -v` passed 10 tests; the combined Day 2-Day 4 regression suite passed 42 tests. The generated FBX roundtripped with 89/89 bones, 661/661 vertices, 89/89 frames, no leaf bones, Z-up/-Y-forward axis metadata, and frame-0 rotation max delta `1.3700886175582738e-07`. A direct `ghostrigger_export_unity_fbx` dispatch smoke returned a 1309-byte PASS payload. Live KOTOR install remained out of scope and was not written to.

- Added Sprint 1 Day 3B and 3B.5 mesh rebinding with skeleton pre-alignment. The new loader extracts KotOR skin meshes from the isolated stock corpus, resolves skin-local MDX palette slots into global skeleton indices, and normalizes vertices into canonical world space. The rebinder remaps weights to Quinn, handles twist redistribution, preserves a raw-transplant debug mode, and now defaults to `skeleton_aligner.py`, which builds a Quinn-named Aurora-proportioned bind skeleton before the transplant. The raw Day 3B parity failure is documented and the aligned Day 3B.5 parity gates pass.
  Affected areas: `src/core/retargeting/mesh_loader.py`, `src/core/retargeting/mesh_rebinder.py`, `src/core/retargeting/skeleton_aligner.py`, `src/core/retargeting/__init__.py`, `tests/test_mesh_loader.py`, `tests/test_mesh_rebinder.py`, `tests/test_skeleton_aligner.py`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/twist_segment_maps/ue5_manny.json`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/ground_truth/day3b_20260520_230725Z/`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/ground_truth/day3b5_20260521_005604Z/`, `knowledge_base/retargeting/audits/mesh_rebinder_parity_20260520.md`.

Raw pmbam parity failed as expected at 5.3948% mean / 7.3790% p95; aligned pmbam passed at ~3.75e-07% mean / ~2.15e-06% p95, and aligned pfbam passed at 0.2537% mean / 1.6592% p95. Live KOTOR install was read only; no write commands were issued against `C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\swkotor`.

- Added Sprint 1 Day 3A's canonical retargeting coordinate normalizer and pure animation bake core. The normalizer converts GhostRigger XYZW node rotations into WXYZ, accumulates canonical world-space bind matrices, and cross-checks against the audited G5 `MatrixPaletteUploader` inverse-bind cache. The baker computes source-to-target bind offsets, preserves same-skeleton identity retargets, bakes target-shaped `SampledClip` output, and derives first-pass Quinn twist bone rotations without adding any mesh or FBX work yet. No roadmap task ID applies; this is a retargeting sprint gate.

- Added the Sprint 1 Day 2 fixed-rate retargeting sampler on top of the isolated stock corpus. The sampler resolves supermodel chains through the fixture corpus, samples local-space TRS arrays with rotations stored as WXYZ, records first-wins controller ownership for diagnostics, and calls the existing G5 `MatrixPaletteUploader` path once per sampled frame instead of introducing a parallel bake formula. The Day 2 audit also records that stock KOTOR clip frame zero is not guaranteed to equal the static bind pose, so tests assert parity with `AnimationEngine.evaluate(0.0)`.

- Added a modular generated lightmap baking system for the Qt lighting workflow. The baker validates existing UV/lightmap UV channels, rasterizes UV triangles into texel buffers, solves direct lighting from Aurora/dynamic/generated lights, supports optional shadow rays, applies padding/dilation, writes PNG/TGA/JPG textures plus a JSON manifest, runs through a Qt worker dialog, previews generated maps in the viewport without overwriting original KOTOR lightmaps, and exposes generated assignments to export sidecar metadata. No roadmap task ID applies.

  Follow-up: changed scene-load defaults so inherited KOTOR lightmaps are disabled by default and the viewport starts in dynamic scene-lighting mode. The baker's extra ambient contribution is now opt-in so generated lightmaps are driven by actual scene light objects and mesh UVs unless the user explicitly adds ambient fill.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/lighting/settings.py src/gui/lighting/lightmap_controller.py src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py src/gui/lighting/lightmap_bake_settings.py src/gui/dialogs/qt_lightmap_baker_dialog.py tests/test_core_contracts.py tests/test_lightmap_baker.py`; `pytest tests/test_lightmap_baker.py tests/test_core_contracts.py -q`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`.
  Follow-up: vectorized the lightmap direct-light solve path so valid texels are lit in NumPy batches instead of per-texel Python loops. This keeps output equivalent to the scalar solver while reducing CPU overhead; shadow rays remain a separate CPU fallback until a UV-space GPU framebuffer baker is implemented.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/lighting/lightmap_lighting_solver.py tests/test_lightmap_baker.py`; `pytest tests/test_lightmap_baker.py -q`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`.
  Follow-up: added a ModernGL offscreen GPU direct-light backend for generated lightmap bakes, enabled by default through a new `GPU acceleration` dialog option. Direct lighting now uploads rasterized texel buffers through RAM to GPU textures, processes large light lists in chunks without dropping lights, accumulates the result before tone/gamma, and reports whether the GPU or CPU fallback was used. Optional shadow rays still run on the CPU and automatically bypass the GPU path when enabled.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/lighting/lightmap_gpu_solver.py src/gui/lighting/lightmap_bake_settings.py src/gui/lighting/lightmap_bake_job.py src/gui/lighting/lightmap_baker.py src/gui/dialogs/qt_lightmap_baker_dialog.py tests/test_lightmap_baker.py`; `pytest tests/test_lightmap_baker.py -q`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`; local bake probe confirmed `GPU direct-light solve used ModernGL backend 'default'` and produced non-flat output.

No roadmap task ID applies.

- Added a first-pass Aurora lighting system for realistic viewport rendering. Binary light controllers now populate radius, color, and multiplier on `ModelNode` light nodes; the GPU shader can render Aurora-backed point, spot, directional, and area-style lights with distance falloff; the viewport exposes Scene Lit, Unlit, and Studio lighting modes plus per-map toggles for diffuse, lightmap, environment, specular, and normal map channels; and the main window has a dockable Lighting panel for inspecting and editing individual Aurora lights. No roadmap task ID applies.
  Affected areas: `src/core/model_data.py`, `src/core/kotor_loader.py`, `src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py`, `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/panels/qt_lighting_panel.py`, `src/gui/qt_lib.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`, `tests/test_qt_only_imports.py`, `tests/test_regression.py`.

Module lightmaps now stay on the baked-lighting path instead of adding live Aurora scene lights on top of the baked map.

Lighting editor refreshes now preserve the selected light instead of jumping back to the first light after a radius or property edit. No roadmap task ID applies.

- Explicitly releases GPU and RAM-side mesh buffers when clearing the current model or replacing it with another model/module. Model loading now drives the existing progress toast through file/resource read, parse, RAM buffer preparation, UI handoff, and GPU upload phases so long mesh loads happen behind the visible progress notification instead of looking frozen.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_static_mesh_prebuild_uses_ram_and_chunked_uploads tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_model_load_worker_uses_single_read_and_gpu_prebuild tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes -q`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`.
  Follow-up: removed the remaining `Handing model to viewport` UI stall by reusing worker-prepared render bounds, deferring TXI metadata on big/module models, caching the `ResourceManager` instead of rebuilding it during handoff, and preventing the GPU paint path from synchronously decoding archive textures. A timing probe for `K1:m01aa_01a` reduced main-window handoff from about 5.2 seconds to about 0.03 seconds.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_static_mesh_prebuild_uses_ram_and_chunked_uploads tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_model_load_worker_uses_single_read_and_gpu_prebuild tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes -q`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`; manual offscreen timing probe for `K1:m01aa_01a`.
  Follow-up: restored realistic-mode texture streaming after the non-blocking handoff change. Background prewarm now gathers diffuse, lightmap, environment, specular, bump, and per-face texture names from renderable geometry nodes, refreshes the GPU viewport as texture batches become resident, and reruns after deferred TXI metadata reveals companion textures.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_realistic_texture_prewarm_loads_detail_textures_without_paint_stall tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_model_load_worker_uses_single_read_and_gpu_prebuild -q`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_static_mesh_prebuild_uses_ram_and_chunked_uploads tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_model_load_worker_uses_single_read_and_gpu_prebuild tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_realistic_texture_prewarm_loads_detail_textures_without_paint_stall -q`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`.
  Follow-up: selection now drives the viewport orbit/zoom pivot. Selecting a mesh, clicked face, or node recenters the arcball target while preserving the current eye position, and `Z` frames the selected face/mesh/node bounds before falling back to full-model framing.
  Verification: `python -m py_compile src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_selection_becomes_orbit_and_z_frame_pivot -q`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_exposes_mesh_multiselect_box_and_ctrl_a tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_context_menu_does_not_pick_on_right_click tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_disables_gpu_culling_for_cpu_parity tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_selection_becomes_orbit_and_z_frame_pivot tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_viewport_navigation_profiles_are_available -q`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`.

- Reduced UI stalls during model/mesh loading by keeping local MDL reads in RAM, preparing static GPU mesh buffers on the loader thread, reusing those prebuilt arrays during GPU rendering, and chunking first-frame GPU buffer uploads so the Qt event loop can continue between batches. Editing a node now invalidates both the RAM-prebuilt mesh data and GPU buffers for the affected subtree. Restored the lazy `src.gui.gpu_renderer` compatibility alias used by the existing GPU contract test, and kept unknown `GHOSTRIGGER_SKIN_FORMULA` values on the documented G5 fallback instead of slipping into the no-qBone FBX fallback.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py`, `src/gui/__init__.py`, `src/core/gpu_skinning.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_uses_overlay_not_cpu_textured_fallback tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_exposes_module_render_modes_and_selection_tint tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_static_mesh_prebuild_uses_ram_and_chunked_uploads tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_model_load_worker_uses_single_read_and_gpu_prebuild -q`; `pytest tests/test_regression.py::test_skin_node_palette_env_switch_unknown_value_falls_back_to_G5 tests/test_regression.py::test_skin_node_palette_without_qbones_uses_hierarchy_bind tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_static_mesh_prebuild_uses_ram_and_chunked_uploads tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_model_load_worker_uses_single_read_and_gpu_prebuild -q`; `python -m py_compile src/core/gpu_skinning.py src/gui/__init__.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`; `pytest tests/ -m "not slow" -x`.

## 2026-05-19

- Fixed K1 module room loading/render hardening for `m02aa_01a`. The model is
  valid game data (`effect`, 127 nodes, 56 meshes) but includes module geometry
  that may not carry ordinary character-style UV arrays, so the viewport render
  timer now catches render failures and falls back from GPU to CPU instead of
  letting the Qt event callback take down the app. Also repaired the MCP render
  helper's grouped-GUI import path so a freshly loaded MCP server can render
  through the current `src/gui/rendering` layout.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`,
  `src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py`, `src/kotormcp/tools/ghostrigger.py`,
  `src/kotormcp/tools/debug_materials.py`, `tests/test_regression.py`.
  Verification: MCP `ghostrigger_model_info`/`ghostrigger_audit` for
  `k1:m02aa_01a`; `python -m pytest tests/test_regression.py::test_gpu_vbo_handles_module_mesh_without_uvs tests/test_regression.py::test_k1_m02aa_01a_module_model_loads_and_renders_without_crashing -q`;
  `python -m pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py::test_gui_root_only_keeps_central_qt_lib tests/test_qt_only_imports.py::test_qt_lib_facade_imports_grouped_modules -q`;
  direct local MCP handler render for `k1:m02aa_01a`; `python scripts/full_scan.py --game k1 --tier modules --category modules --max-models 10 --timeout 30`.
- Added authoritative KOTOR module location labels keyed by module filename and
  room-model stem, including K1 prefixless room models such as `m02aa_01a` and
  K2 room stems such as `201tel_01a`. The Game Library Module tab now enriches
  rows with `module_code`, `location`, `area_name`, and a human-readable
  `area_label`, uses those labels in the filter dropdown, and shows them in
  model list rows/tooltips.
  Affected areas: `src/core/module_categories.py`,
  `src/gui/panels/qt_library_panel.py`, `tests/test_module_categories.py`.

  `python -m py_compile src/core/module_categories.py src/gui/panels/qt_library_panel.py tests/test_module_categories.py`.
- Added a dedicated `MODULE` Character Mode for area/module/tile/effect models. Module classifications now show a `MODULE` badge instead of `UNSUPPORTED`, with its own UI color and enum display/icon key.
  Affected areas: `src/core/model_data.py`, `src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py`, `tests/test_character_mode.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_character_mode.py::test_detect_character_mode_synthetic tests/test_character_mode.py::test_character_mode_has_required_members -q`; `python -m py_compile src/core/model_data.py src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py tests/test_character_mode.py`.
- Added module-mesh inspection and visibility controls for imported modules. The Properties panel now has a `Module Meshes` tab listing mesh name, vertex/face counts, texture, visibility, and mesh group; selecting a mesh row selects/highlights it in the viewport. The viewport now supports mesh picking, a 3ds Max-style right-click mesh menu for hide/unhide/hide-unselected/unhide-all plus selection set and mesh group creation, and GPU-only `Realistic`, `Shaded`, and `Flat` render modes. Hidden module meshes are skipped by both CPU and GPU render paths; gray-mesh auto-hide is intentionally left for the next classification pass now that mesh inspection exists.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py`, `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py`, `src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py`, `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_exposes_module_render_modes_and_selection_tint tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_lists_selects_and_hides_meshes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/rendering/viewport_core.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: `Realistic`, `Shaded`, and `Flat` now all keep diffuse texture sampling when Texture is enabled. Mesh selection now supports multi-select from the Properties list, Ctrl+A select-all in the viewport, front-facing triangle preference for click picking, and viewport drag-box selection for selecting multiple module meshes.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_exposes_module_render_modes_and_selection_tint tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_lists_selects_and_hides_meshes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_supports_multi_select_all tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_exposes_mesh_multiselect_box_and_ctrl_a tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: right-click no longer changes mesh selection or acts like a left-click picker. The context menu opens only when the cursor is over an already selected mesh, and hide/unhide actions operate on the current selected mesh set.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_context_menu_does_not_pick_on_right_click tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_exposes_mesh_multiselect_box_and_ctrl_a -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: hiding selected meshes now keeps the selected mesh set, and the context menu can open from an existing selection even if the clicked point is empty or the selected meshes are hidden. Right-click still does not pick or replace selection; it only acts on the current selected set.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_context_menu_does_not_pick_on_right_click -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: the viewport right-click menu now opens even when no mesh is selected, keeping global actions such as `Unhide All` available. Selection-specific actions stay disabled until meshes are selected, and right-click still does not pick or replace selection.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_context_menu_does_not_pick_on_right_click -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: the module mesh browser and viewport selection now include any node with real vertices and faces, even if the loader did not classify it as a formal mesh node. This exposes gray module/helper geometry in `Module Meshes` so it can be selected and hidden.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_includes_geometry_nodes_outside_mesh_nodes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_supports_multi_select_all tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_viewport_exposes_mesh_multiselect_box_and_ctrl_a -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: the module geometry browser now separates normal `Meshes` from `Walkmeshes` in a nested tab control. Walkmesh/AABB geometry is still selectable and hideable, but no longer mixed into the normal module mesh list.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_splits_meshes_and_walkmeshes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_supports_multi_select_all -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: the module geometry browser now adds a `NULL Meshes` tab for geometry whose texture is empty, `NULL`, `none`, or `****`. These external/helper meshes remain selectable and hideable, but are no longer mixed into the normal `Meshes` tab.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_splits_meshes_nulls_and_walkmeshes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_supports_multi_select_all -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: added a detachable/dockable `Module Meshes` panel opened from `Modules -> Open Module Meshes`. It mirrors the module geometry browser, stays synchronized with viewport selection/visibility, and can float or dock like the Nodes, 2DA, and Resource panels.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_exposes_module_meshes_as_detachable_dock tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_splits_meshes_nulls_and_walkmeshes -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: added an `Open Window` button inside the embedded Module Meshes tab and a right-click `Open Module Meshes Window` action on the mesh/NULL/walkmesh lists, so the detachable dock can be opened directly from the module browser.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_panel_can_request_detachable_window tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_exposes_module_meshes_as_detachable_dock -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: removed the `Module Meshes` tab from the regular Properties sidebar now that module geometry has a detachable/dockable panel. The dock remains synchronized with viewport mesh selection and visibility, while the sidebar returns to the simpler `General` properties view.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_regular_properties_panel_can_omit_module_mesh_tab tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_lists_selects_and_hides_meshes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_supports_multi_select_all tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_properties_panel_splits_meshes_nulls_and_walkmeshes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_module_mesh_panel_can_request_detachable_window tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_exposes_module_meshes_as_detachable_dock -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: module `Shaded` mode now uses a soft diffuse-texture-preserving shade instead of feeding textures through lightmap/spec/env detail maps. Module `Realistic` also uses that soft shaded path until explicit scene lights are available, while `Flat` remains raw diffuse texture. Detail maps remain reserved for non-module realistic rendering.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_exposes_module_render_modes_and_selection_tint -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Follow-up: module Flat/Shaded modes now force room geometry into the opaque pass, ignore diffuse alpha for those modes, and keep selected module nodes two-sided/opaque. Shaded uses a stronger but still texture-preserving normal shade so it differs from Flat without triggering the slow transparent composite path.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_exposes_module_render_modes_and_selection_tint tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes -q`; `python -m py_compile src/gui/rendering/gpu_renderer.py tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
- Added `CHEETSHEET.md` as the maintained reference for useful embedded Python terminal commands, starting with selected-model inspection, animation playback, animation override, viewport, and log examples. Updated `AGENTS.md` so future terminal helper changes also update the cheatsheet.
  Affected areas: `CHEETSHEET.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGES.md`.
  Verification: documentation-only change; reviewed the new cheatsheet commands against the current terminal helper bindings.
- Added Python terminal helper bindings for the main window's selected model and animation controls. The embedded terminal now exposes `window`, `main_window`, `viewport()`, `model()`, `selected_model()`, `animation_names()`, `select_animation(name)`, `play_animation(name, loop=None)`, `stop_animation()`, `seek_animation(percent)`, and `override_animation(target_name, source_name="", source_model=None)`.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/windows/qt_main_window.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_exposes_animation_helpers_to_python_terminal tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_bottom_area_is_resizable_splitter -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\windows\qt_main_window.py tests\test_core_contracts.py`.
- Fixed the Matrix background Aurebesh font lookup after GUI category grouping. It now loads AurebeshAF from the shared `src/gui/fonts/AurebeshAF` directory instead of the nonexistent `src/gui/assets/fonts/AurebeshAF` path.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/assets/qt_matrix_background.py`, `tests/test_qt_only_imports.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py::test_matrix_background_uses_bundled_aurebesh_font_dir tests/test_qt_only_imports.py::test_qt_lib_facade_imports_grouped_modules -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\assets\qt_matrix_background.py tests\test_qt_only_imports.py`.

  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_bottom_area_is_resizable_splitter tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_main_window_combines_animation_tabs_and_removes_builder_tab -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\windows\qt_main_window.py tests\test_core_contracts.py`.
- Removed the embedded Builder tab from the main right-side tab stack because Character Builder already opens from Tools. Combined the former `Anims` and `Anim Lib` tabs into one `Animation Library` tab with internal `Current` and `Library` pages, while keeping the existing animation panel objects and callbacks intact. Blueprint module actions now open the standalone Blueprint Editor window instead of trying to select a removed tab.

- Moved the Diagnostics, Texture, Normal, and Blueprint utility panels out of the main right-side tab stack. Diagnostics now opens as `Tools -> Diagnostics...`, Texture and Normal now share `Tools -> Texture Tool...`, and Blueprint Editor now opens as `Tools -> Blueprint Editor...`; existing `self.*_panel` references remain pointed at the embedded panels so current callbacks keep working.

- Moved the Rig UI out of the main right-side tab stack and into a standalone Rigging window opened from `Modules -> Open Rigging Window`. The main window keeps `self.rig_panel` pointed at the window's embedded panel so existing Auto-Rig, Remove Rigging, Weight Stats, and status update paths continue to work.

- Split the bottom Output Log panel into a horizontal log/terminal workspace. The existing log stays on the left, and the right side now hosts an embedded Python terminal with command history, Run/Copy/Clear controls, stdout/stderr capture, expression display, and access to Qt plus the log panel context.
  Updated follow-up: moved the Output Log Save/Copy/Clear controls into a footer under the log pane, and moved the Python Run/Copy/Clear controls into a footer under the terminal pane, so each pane owns its own buttons.

- Restored the original joint-dot fill palette while keeping the new key-joint marking as a blue accent ring instead of replacing the dot color. Also added `shin` to the side-name palette so KotOR lower-leg names such as `lshin_g` keep their side color.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_color_constants_match_spec tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_accurig_left_names_classify_red tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_accurig_right_names_classify_green tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_tokenised_side_naming tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_center_spine_names_classify_cyan tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_default_center_classification tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_hip_is_center_not_side tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_key_joints_keep_original_fill_palette tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_key_joint_names_are_marked_for_blue_accent tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t401_non_key_joints_keep_original_palette -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\viewports\qt_viewport.py tests\test_joint_dot_overlay.py`.
- Added a `HUMANOID` character mode for full humanoid NPC/body models that have a humanoid skeleton and their own visible head/facial rig, so models such as Bith-style NPCs and Calo Nord-style bodies no longer classify as `HEAD`. The Character Builder toolbar, property badge, workflow rail, scene priority, and viewport camera preset now understand the new mode. Also narrowed blue joint-dot highlighting to anatomical key joints only (head, neck, shoulder/clavicle, spine/torso, elbow/forearm, hand, knee/shin, and foot), while ordinary roots, pelvis, thighs, fingers, and other nodes keep the original center/side palette.
  Affected areas: `src/core/model_data.py`, `src/gui/viewports/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/panels/qt_character_builder_panel.py`, `src/gui/panels/qt_properties_panel.py`, `src/gui/panels/qt_workflow_rail.py`, `tests/test_character_mode.py`, `tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py`.

- Fixed Qt icon asset resolution after GUI category grouping. The main window, shared theme, and icon manager now resolve tab arrows and PNG icons from the shared `src/gui/icons` directory instead of looking for nonexistent `windows/icons` or `assets/icons` folders, and the Qt import guard now checks those paths.

- Rerouted the remaining application entry points, scripts, IPC reload defaults, and texture-loading helper imports through the central `src.gui.qt_lib` facade. `main.py` now launches via `src.gui.qt_lib.windows.qt_main_window`, diagnostic/render scripts use `src.gui.qt_lib.rendering.*`, and the facade aliases now support `importlib.reload()` for hot-reload workflows. Updated `AGENTS.md` and added a repository import guard so deleted root GUI modules are not reintroduced by runnable code.

- Replaced the temporary root-level `src/gui/*.py` compatibility shim pile with a single central `src/gui/qt_lib.py` facade. The root `src/gui` Python files are now only `qt_lib.py` and `__init__.py`, while the implementation modules remain in category folders. Application and test imports were rerouted through `src.gui.qt_lib.<category>.<module>`, and the Qt import guard now fails if root shim modules return.

Updated the Qt-only import guard to recurse through the new subpackages and documented the layout in `src/gui/README.md`.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/`, `tests/test_qt_only_imports.py`, `src/gui/README.md`.
  Verification: imported old and new GUI module paths with `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen`; `pytest tests/test_qt_only_imports.py tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_uses_overlay_not_cpu_textured_fallback tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_exposes_reload_code_button tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_animation_selection_arms_preview_pose -q`; `pytest tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t403_thumbnail_visibility_is_opt_in tests/test_joint_dot_overlay.py::test_t405_gradient_endpoints -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui tests\test_qt_only_imports.py`.
- Changed GPU wireframe rendering to use a 3ds Max-style cyan-blue line color instead of inheriting each mesh's material or texture color. The fragment shader now has an explicit wireframe color path used by wire-only and GPU wire overlay passes.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/gpu_renderer.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\gpu_renderer.py tests\test_core_contracts.py`.
- Kept Qt viewports on the GPU for wireframe and texture-disabled display modes instead of falling back to CPU rendering. The GPU renderer now tracks `show_solid` and `show_texture`, skips texture sampling when textures are disabled, uses GPU wireframe mode for wire views, and the Qt overlay no longer repaints wireframe through the CPU on top of a GPU frame.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/qt_viewport.py`, `src/gui/gpu_renderer.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_uses_overlay_not_cpu_textured_fallback tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_keeps_gpu_for_wire_and_texture_off_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_gpu_renderer_supports_texture_off_and_wireframe_modes tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_qt_gpu_viewport_disables_gpu_culling_for_cpu_parity tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_uses_gpu_during_animation_preview -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\qt_viewport.py src\gui\gpu_renderer.py tests\test_core_contracts.py`.
- Fixed Unreal Animator target animation preview for imported Quinn FBX skins by making qBone-less GPU skin meshes use hierarchy inverse-bind matrices instead of identity qBone/tBone fallbacks, allowing imported world-space skin meshes to use LBS when an animation pose is active, and forcing both Unreal Animator viewports onto GPU while animation preview is playing before restoring the user's previous GPU/CPU choices on Stop.
  Affected areas: `src/core/gpu_skinning.py`, `src/gui/viewport_core.py`, `src/gui/qt_unreal_animator.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`, `tests/test_regression.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_regression.py::test_skin_node_palette_restores_3f_qbone_tbone_path tests/test_regression.py::test_skin_node_palette_without_qbones_uses_hierarchy_bind tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_uses_gpu_during_animation_preview tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_animation_selection_arms_preview_pose -q`; `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_inserts_synthetic_spine_between_pelvis_and_torso tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_inserts_synthetic_spine_when_pelvis_and_torso_share_rootdummy tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_repositions_existing_spine_between_pelvis_and_torso tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_source_bone_browser_lists_and_selects_spine tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_manually_adds_and_deletes_source_synthetic_bones tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_exposes_reload_code_button tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_animation_selection_arms_preview_pose tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_uses_gpu_during_animation_preview -q`.
- Added a developer-facing Unreal Animator `Reload Code` button and `Ctrl+Shift+R` action that asks the Qt main window to reload the Unreal Animator Python modules, rebuild the window, restore its geometry, refresh the supermodel library, and reload the last selected source supermodel when available.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/qt_unreal_animator.py`, `src/gui/qt_main_window.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_exposes_reload_code_button tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_manually_adds_and_deletes_source_synthetic_bones -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\qt_unreal_animator.py src\gui\qt_main_window.py tests\test_core_contracts.py`.
- Added Unreal Animator source-supermodel preparation that dynamically inserts or repositions a `spine_g` joint between `pelvis_g` and `torso_g` when a KotOR animation supermodel lacks that real intermediary. Added a KotOR source bone browser tab so source bones, including the synthetic `spine_g`, can be inspected and selected. Updated Unreal aliasing so `spine_g` maps to Unreal `spine_01` and `torso_g` maps higher in the spine chain.
  Affected areas: `src/gui/qt_unreal_animator.py`, `src/unreal/animation_retargeting.py`, `tests/test_core_contracts.py`.
  Updated follow-up: the source-supermodel spine repair now also handles real KotOR layouts where `pelvis_g` and `torso_g` are siblings under a hidden `rootdummy`, rewiring the visible path to `pelvis_g -> spine_g -> torso_g` while keeping `rootdummy` hidden.
  Corrected follow-up: the KotOR source skeleton remains authoritative. The Unreal Animator no longer synthesizes the full 89-bone Quinn skeleton or hides KotOR-only source bones. It now removes stale broad Quinn synthetic nodes automatically, keeps `spine_g` as the known automatic source fix, and exposes manual `Add Synthetic`, `Insert Before`, and `Delete Synthetic` controls in the source bone browser. Manual synthetic deletion reparents children back to the KotOR parent while preserving world placement. Selecting an animation still arms frame-zero preview immediately.
  Verification: `pytest tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_inserts_synthetic_spine_between_pelvis_and_torso tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_inserts_synthetic_spine_when_pelvis_and_torso_share_rootdummy tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_repositions_existing_spine_between_pelvis_and_torso tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_source_bone_browser_lists_and_selects_spine tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_manually_adds_and_deletes_source_synthetic_bones tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_animator_animation_selection_arms_preview_pose tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_quinn_aliases_map_common_kotor_supermodel_bones tests/test_core_contracts.py::test_unreal_bone_map_excludes_dummy_and_hook_helpers -q`; `python -m compileall -q src\gui\qt_unreal_animator.py src\unreal\animation_retargeting.py tests\test_core_contracts.py`; loaded a shown `QtUnrealAnimatorWindow` with `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen` and confirmed the manual synthetic controls exist, KotOR-only `eyelid` remains visible, a manually inserted source spacer appears between `torso_g` and `neck_g`, and selecting `taunt` arms preview.
